([filext.com](https://filext.com/file-extension/HWPX?utm_source=openai))\n\nThis ZIP-container fact matters because many “sniffers” will initially classify the file as `application/zip` unless they inspect package contents. (This is a common issue for “Office Open XML”-style formats.) ([reddit.com](https://www.reddit.com/r/learnprogramming/comments/pct7xs?utm_source=openai))\n\n## 2) The `mimetype` file inside HWPX and what it contains\nMultiple sources specifically state that an HWPX package contains a top-level file named **`mimetype`** whose content identifies the package’s MIME type:\n\n- A Korean technical note/wiki describing HWPX internal structure says the `mimetype` file records that HWPX is **`application/hwp+zip`**. ([github-wiki-see.page](https://github-wiki-see.page/m/pai-plznw4me/hwp-format-transfer/wiki/hwpx-%EB%82%B4%EB%B6%80%EA%B5%AC%EC%A1%B0?utm_source=openai))\n- The `pyhwpxlib` project (a tool/library for working with HWPX) notes that the **`mimetype` entry is stored uncompressed** (typical “OCF/packaging” convention) and treats `mimetype` as a required component of a valid HWPX package. ([dlm.microfocus.com](https://dlm.microfocus.com/documentation/idol/IDOL_12_5/CFS_12.5_Documentation/Guides/pdf/English/ConnectorFrameworkServer_12.5_Admin_en.pdf?utm_source=openai))\n\nThere is also evidence of ecosystem inconsistency / alternates:\n- A discussion about adding HWPX to `shared-mime-info` references using **`application/x-hwpx`** as a “representative type”, while also noting a return to `hwp+zip` according to the internal `mimetype`. ([groups.google.com](https://groups.google.com/g/hwp-foss/c/8FtrFvVeChk?utm_source=openai))\n\nSo, depending on the platform/database, you may see `application/x-hwpx`, but the **internal package `mimetype` and multiple tools/docs point to `application/hwp+zip`**. commonly used internal package mimetype for HWPX\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{([^{}]+)\\}\\}\")\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass XmlProcessResult:\n    modified: bool\n    xml_text: str\n    replaced_keys: List[str]\n\n\ndef load_event_data(path: Path) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"event_data.json must be an object/dict, got {type(data)}\")\n    # Convert everything to strings (preserve original text; do not normalize)\n    out: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        if not isinstance(k, str):\n            raise ValueError(f\"JSON keys must be strings, got {type(k)}\")\n        out[k] = v if isinstance(v, str) else json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)\n    return out\n\n\ndef _raw_replace(xml_text: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:\n    replaced: List[str] = []\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        token = \"{{\" + k + \"}}\"\n        if token in xml_text:\n            xml_text = xml_text.replace(token, v)\n            replaced.append(k)\n    return xml_text, replaced\n\n\ndef _cross_node_replace(xml_text: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Handle placeholders that may be split across multiple text nodes.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Parse XML with lxml.\n    - For each paragraph-like container (<*p>), concatenate all descendant <*t> texts.\n    - Replace any {{key}} patterns in the concatenated text.\n    - Write the resulting full text into the first <*t> node; clear the rest.\n\n    This is conservative in behavior: it only changes paragraphs whose concatenated\n    text still contains '{{' after the raw pass.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False, recover=False, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_text.encode(\"utf-8\"), parser=parser)\n\n    replaced_keys: List[str] = []\n\n    # Read & process entries first\n        processed: Dict[str, bytes] = {}\n        for info in infos:\n            payload = zin.read(info.filename)\n            if info.filename.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                if b\"{{\" in payload:\n                    res = process_xml(payload, data)\n                    payload = res.xml_text.encode(\"utf-8\")\n                    if res.modified:\n                        modified_xml_files.append(info.filename)\n                    replaced_key_set.update(res.replaced_keys)\n            processed[info.filename] = payload\n\n    # Convert everything to strings (preserve original text; do not normalize)\n    out: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        if not isinstance(k, str):\n            raise ValueError(f\"JSON keys must be strings, got {type(k)}\")\n        out[k] = v if isinstance(v, str) else json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)\n    return out\n\n\ndef _raw_replace(xml_text: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:\n    replaced: List[str] = []\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        token = \"{{\" + k + \"}}\"\n        if token in xml_text:\n            xml_text = xml_text.replace(token, v)\n            replaced.append(k)\n    return xml_text, replaced\n\n\ndef _cross_node_replace(xml_text: str, data: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Handle placeholders that may be split across multiple text nodes.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Parse XML with lxml.\n    - For each paragraph-like container (<*p>), concatenate all descendant <*t> texts.\n    - Replace any {{key}} patterns in the concatenated text.\n    - Write the resulting full text into the first <*t> node; clear the rest.\n\n    This is conservative in behavior: it only changes paragraphs whose concatenated\n    text still contains '{{' after the raw pass.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False, recover=False, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_text.encode(\"utf-8\"), parser=parser)\n\n    replaced_keys: List[str] = []\n\n    # Put all resulting paragraph text into the first <t>; clear others.\n        ts[0].text = new_combined\n        for t in ts[1:]:\n            t.text = \"\"\n\n    xml_out = etree.tostring(\n        root,\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n        xml_declaration=True,\n        standalone=None,\n    ).decode(\"utf-8\")\n    return xml_out, replaced_keys\n\n\ndef _remove_line_seg_array(xml_text: str) -> str:\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False, recover=False, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_text.encode(\"utf-8\"), parser=parser)\n\n    # Read & process entries first\n        processed: Dict[str, bytes] = {}\n        for info in infos:\n            payload = zin.read(info.filename)\n            if info.filename.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                if b\"{{\" in payload:\n                    res = process_xml(payload, data)\n                    payload = res.xml_text.encode(\"utf-8\")\n                    if res.modified:\n                        modified_xml_files.append(info.filename)\n                    replaced_key_set.update(res.replaced_keys)\n            processed[info.filename] = payload\n\n    #
